pub struct BookSide  (one persistent price-ordered bid or ask side, plus its applied deltas; full ordered live depth and deltas participate in its identity)
  impl Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Default, Element, MarketElement for BookSide
  pub fn new(side: Side) -> Result<Self>  (only a bid or ask)
  pub fn live(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = &MarketOperation>  (best price first; same-price entries by stated position)
  pub fn deltas(&self) -> &[MarketOperation]
  pub fn len(&self) -> usize
  pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool
  pub fn best_price(&self) -> Option<Decimal18>
  pub fn best_quantity(&self) -> Option<Decimal18>  (aggregate quantity at the exact best price)
  pub fn add_operation(&mut self, operation: MarketOperation) -> Result<()>  (atomic; order or quote on this side only; live identity is symbol + BookScope + cross identity; a stated same-partition MDEntryRefID resolves before the incoming identity and a distinct occupied destination refuses; FIX change/overlay inherits a matched live price or size only when the corresponding tag was absent and refuses an omitted value without a predecessor; range deletes require a positive in-range same-partition position)
pub struct Book  (one symbol and instant: persistent bid and ask depth plus executions at this effective timestamp)
  impl Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Default, Element, Event, MarketElement for Book
  pub fn new(unix: i64, symbol: impl Into<String>) -> Self
  pub const fn bid(&self) -> &BookSide
  pub const fn ask(&self) -> &BookSide
  pub fn executions(&self) -> &[Execution]
  pub fn is_crossed(&self) -> bool  (best bid strictly above best ask; a locked book is coherent)
  pub fn bbo_midpoint(&self) -> Option<Decimal18>  (overflow-safe arithmetic midpoint of a coherent two-sided BBO)
  pub fn median_quantity(&self) -> Option<Decimal18>  (overflow-safe two-value median of aggregate best quantities; the one side where one-sided)
  pub fn add_operations<I: IntoIterator<Item = MarketOperation>>(&mut self, operations: I) -> Result<()>  (atomic; one nonregressing currunix; advancing clears old deltas/executions/snapunix but retains live depth; full-snapshot orders, quotes and empty controls clear only their symbol + `BookScope` partition while executions never control depth; root sequence/creation/recording/execution clocks are folded from components; executions are reported beside depth and never mutate it; an identity moving sides retires the old side)
  impl AsRef<MarketEventData>, AsMut<MarketEventData> for Book
pub struct BookIterator<I: Iterator<Item = MarketOperation>>  (books from an already sorted operation stream, one per symbol and effective timestamp, or one consolidated global book; each timestamp/symbol commits ordinary deltas, expirations and explicit scope-level snapshot membership atomically; snapshotted executions are reported without replacing depth and future snapshot components are refused rather than backdated; live depth expires one generation at exprtime before equal-time source operations; a failed group yields no stale book)
  pub fn new(operations: I, snapshot_millis: u64, global: bool) -> Result<Self>  (zero disables the epoch-aligned snapshot grid; checked conversion to nanoseconds)
  pub const fn global(&self) -> bool
  impl<I: Iterator<Item = MarketOperation>> Iterator<Item = Result<Book>> for BookIterator<I>
  impl<I: FusedIterator<Item = MarketOperation>> FusedIterator for BookIterator<I>

### yggdryl::graph::iterator  [rust/src/graph/iterator.rs]
pub struct EventIterator<E, I>  (the one walk: events read in their order, a missing `execunix` filled from `currunix` only where `Event::is_execution` says that incoming observation reports an execution, then each stated as the one after the live element it follows and yielded so; the live elements are kept under the identity every incarnation of one thing shares - the cross element, the element's own where it states no cross code - and an element arriving under a live identity, or, where its own is alive under nothing, under a name (scheme, value) a live element goes by, is stated as the one after it by its own `with_previous`; the yielded element stands as the live one where still alive (live state, not past its expiration) and retires the identity where not, so a filled order ends its chain and a later element under its identity starts afresh; one arriving under the identity the live element arrived under - the same instant and content, logged again - is yielded `Event::restating` it, keeping the earliest execution and recording instants, so the chain grows by nothing; one before the live element is yielded as it came and changes nothing, one its reading refuses is yielded as it came and stands; Debug)
  impl<E: Event + Clone, I: Iterator<Item = E>> EventIterator<E, I>
  pub fn new(elements: impl IntoIterator<IntoIter = I>, sorted: bool) -> Self  (`sorted` takes the caller's word that the elements arrive in their own order and streams them; otherwise they are collected and stably sorted by `is_after` / `is_before` first)
  pub const fn with_snapshot_ns(mut self, snapshot_ns: i64) -> Self  (a grid: a step in nanoseconds aligned on the epoch, and the walk then reads one snapshot per step per identity - the first element to reach a step its identity has not consumed is stamped with the step's opening instant as its snapunix, every later one in that step with none; zero or less takes the grid away, and without one the instant is left as it came)
  pub fn snapshot_ns(&self) -> Option<i64>  (the grid step, where there is one)
  pub fn alive(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = &E>  (the elements still alive after what was read, one per identity, in no order)
  impl<E: Event + Clone, I: Iterator<Item = E>> Iterator for EventIterator<E, I>  (Item = E; size_hint is the source's)
  impl<E: Event + Clone, I: FusedIterator<Item = E>> FusedIterator for EventIterator<E, I>

### yggdryl::txhash::value  [rust/src/txhash/value.rs]
pub const UNIX_WIDTH: usize = 8;
  pub const fn width(algorithm: DigestAlgorithm) -> usize
  pub const fn dtype(algorithm: DigestAlgorithm) -> DataType
  pub struct TxHash
  pub const fn new(unix: i64, digest: Digest) -> Self
  pub fn new_in(unix: i64, unit: TimeUnit, digest: Digest) -> Result<Self>
  pub const fn unix(&self) -> i64
  pub const fn unit(&self) -> TimeUnit
  pub const fn digest(&self) -> Digest
  pub const fn algorithm(&self) -> DigestAlgorithm
  pub const fn width(&self) -> usize
  pub const fn dtype(&self) -> DataType
  pub fn with_unit(self, unit: TimeUnit) -> Result<Self>
  pub fn into_uuid(self) -> Result<crate::Uuid>  (RFC 9562 UUIDv7: the instant is restated and floored directly to signed i64 microseconds and packed as the millisecond it falls in with the microsecond within it in rand_a's high ten, and the digest is stored whole behind it - its top two bits above the variant, its low sixty-two below; nothing is hashed a second time and nothing is narrowed, so the projection is lossless and two values share an identifier only where their microsecond and all 64 digest bits agree; requires a 64-bit digest; checked unit conversion; refuses an instant before the epoch or past the microsecond count the 48-bit millisecond timestamp reaches at `$`; zero successful allocations; raw TxHash bytes and ordering unchanged)
  pub fn into_ordered_bytes(self) -> Result<[u8; 16]>  (the same ordering with no layout: sign-flipped signed i64 microseconds big-endian in 0..8, all 64 digest bits in 8..16; requires a 64-bit digest; checked unit conversion; what the FIX identity columns hold; Rust-only)
  pub fn into_bytes(self) -> TxHashBytes
  pub fn from_bytes(unit: TimeUnit, algorithm: DigestAlgorithm, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<Self>
  pub fn from_str(value: &str) -> Result<Self>
  pub fn into_datetime(self) -> Scalar
  pub fn into_scalar(self) -> Scalar
  pub fn from_scalar(unit: TimeUnit, algorithm: DigestAlgorithm, value: &Scalar) -> Result<Self>
  pub fn stable_hash(&self) -> u64
  pub struct TxHashBytes
  impl Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash, Display, FromStr, Serialize, Deserialize for TxHash  (Ord is unit-major, then signed count, then digest; instants are not normalized across units)

  pub fn encode_value_stream_bytes(&self) -> ValueStream  (the value stream one chunk per leaf: the version and the id, then the payload, then a nested value's children; on Scalar; DataType and every DataTypeValue answer `encode_value_stream_bytes(&self, value: &Scalar) -> Result<ValueStream>`, the value cast first)
  pub fn into_value_bytes(&self) -> Vec<u8>  (the same bytes whole; DataType and every DataTypeValue answer `encode_value_bytes(&self, value: &Scalar) -> Result<Vec<u8>>`)
  pub fn decode_value_bytes(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<Self>  (the value one stream holds, refused at the byte: another version, a byte naming no datatype or a family's placeholder, a payload cut short, bytes left over; DataType and every DataTypeValue answer `decode_value_bytes(&self, bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<Scalar>`, cast to the datatype)
  pub fn decode_value_stream_bytes<I>(chunks: I) -> Result<Self> where I: IntoIterator, I::Item: AsRef<[u8]>  (the chunks gathered and read as one; DataType and every DataTypeValue answer the same over `&self`)
  pub fn into_variant(&self) -> Result<Variant>  (on Scalar: the value as the Parquet Variant encoding; `Scalar::from_variant(&Variant)` reads it back, and DataType answers `encode_variant`/`decode_variant` with the cast)
  pub fn digest(&self, algorithm: DigestAlgorithm) -> Digest  (on Scalar, FieldScalar<'_>, and FieldRecord<'_>)
  pub struct ValueBytes<'a>

### yggdryl::holder  [rust/src/holder/mod.rs]
pub enum Holder
  Buffer(Buffer)
  LocalFolder(crate::local::LocalFolder), LocalPath(crate::local::LocalPath), LocalFile(crate::local::LocalFile)
  FsFolder(crate::fs::FsFolder), FsPath(crate::fs::FsPath), FsFile(crate::fs::FsFile)
  S3Folder(crate::s3::S3Folder), S3Path(crate::s3::S3Path), S3File(crate::s3::S3File)  (behind the `s3` feature)
  ZipNode(crate::zip::ZipNode), ZipPath(crate::zip::ZipPath), ZipLeaf(crate::zip::ZipLeaf)
  Buffered(Box<Buffered<Self>>), Coded(Box<Coded>), Text(Box<crate::text::Text<Self>>), Media(Box<crate::media::Media>)  (each wraps another Holder, so each is boxed)
  pub const fn buffer(buffer: Buffer) -> Self
  pub fn folder(path: impl AsRef<std::path::Path>) -> Result<Self>
  pub fn file(path: impl AsRef<std::path::Path>) -> Result<Self>
  pub fn local(path: impl AsRef<std::path::Path>) -> Result<Self>
  pub fn from_url<K, V>(url: &Url, properties: impl IntoIterator<Item = (K, V)>) -> Result<Self> where
  pub fn zip(handle: Self) -> Self
  pub fn buffered(self, options: BufferedOptions) -> Self
  pub fn into_media(self) -> Self
  pub fn into_declared_media(self) -> Self
  pub fn open(&mut self) -> Result<()>
  pub fn into_text(self) -> Self
  pub fn into_text_with(self, options: crate::text::TextOptions) -> Self
  pub fn into_coded(self) -> Self
  pub fn into_coded_with(self, codec: crate::Codec, level: crate::Level) -> Self
  pub fn as_io(&self) -> &dyn IOBase
  pub fn as_io_mut(&mut self) -> &mut dyn IOBase

### yggdryl::text::line  [rust/src/text/line.rs]
pub struct TextLine  (one text row and the `Event` of the graph it is: what the reader cut - the index, the identifier it was read under - answered as a location where it is one and resolved to one where it is a name - the handle's modification time, the whole body with the row header included, the edges stripped and the byte limit applied - and the shared `Arc<TextOptions>` it reads itself by; every other fact is a reading of the body under the options, resolved on its first ask, once, into a slot of its own, so a caller reading the body and the row number resolves nothing else; a `set_` states a fact over the line's own reading, and `set_body` drops every resolved slot; the body is never empty, refused at both doors that set one, which is why the `body` column a read answers is not nullable; equality, order and hash read the stated facts alone)
  pub fn from_bytes(index: u64, body: TextBytes, options: Arc<TextOptions>) -> Result<Self>  (where the bytes become text: what is not UTF-8 is decoded once, each byte as its Windows-1252 character, and counted; an empty body is refused, naming the line, because a line is the line it holds)
  pub const fn index(&self) -> u64
  pub fn set_index(&mut self, index: u64)
  pub fn options(&self) -> &TextOptions
  pub fn sourceuri(&self) -> Option<&Uri>  (the identifier the line was read under - what the handle is addressed by, which is not always a place: a read through a name answers that name here; shared with every other line of that read, and what the cross code spells, so the code does not move with the directory a name resolved in)
  pub fn sourceurl(&self) -> Option<&Url>  (where the line was read from: the same value read as a location, which a located read - the common case - spells with the same text, and where a name resolves to by Uri::locator, which roots a URN's spelled path in the process working directory; none where the identifier resolves nowhere)
  pub const fn shared_url(&self) -> Option<&Arc<Url>>
  pub fn set_sourceuri(&mut self, uri: Option<Arc<Uri>>)  (the identifier is narrowed - and a name resolved, one working-directory read - once here rather than once per row, so `sourceurl` answers it where it is a location and where it resolves to where it is a name; refreshes the derived cross code, cross hash and the two identities)
  pub fn with_sourceuri(mut self, uri: Arc<Uri>) -> Self
  pub const fn handle_mtime(&self) -> Option<i64>  (the modification time of the handle the line was read from, nanoseconds UTC: what `mtime` falls back to)
  pub fn set_handle_mtime(&mut self, mtime: Option<i64>)
  pub fn with_handle_mtime(mut self, mtime: i64) -> Self
  pub fn bodytype(&self) -> &MimeType  (the stated classification, else the payload's, read on the first ask)
  pub fn set_bodytype(&mut self, bodytype: Option<MimeType>)  (unsaid, it resolves afresh)
  pub fn with_bodytype(mut self, bodytype: MimeType) -> Self
  pub fn body(&self) -> &str  (the whole line, row header included)
  pub const fn body_bytes(&self) -> &TextBytes
  pub fn set_body(&mut self, body: TextBytes) -> Result<()>  (drops every resolved reading and the header the cut matched; captures a caller stated stand, the payload then the whole new body, and so does every other stated fact; an empty body is refused and leaves the line as it was)
  pub const fn decoded_byte_size(&self) -> u64
  pub const fn dropped_byte_size(&self) -> Option<u64>
  pub const fn set_dropped_byte_size(&mut self, size: Option<u64>)
  pub fn captures(&self) -> &[Option<TextBytes>]  (the row header matched over the body on the first ask, one slot per declared capture in the order the expression declares them, each a range of the body's page; every slot empty where the header did not match, none under no header)
  pub fn capture(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&str>
  pub fn set_captures(&mut self, captures: Vec<Option<TextBytes>>) -> Result<()>  (the line's word over its header, decoded as a body is)
  pub fn with_captures(mut self, captures: Vec<Option<TextBytes>>) -> Result<Self>
  pub fn entries(&self) -> Option<&TextEntries>  (the stated tree, else the payload's own by `TextEntries::from_bytes` on the first ask; `None` where it states no pair)
  pub fn entries_mut(&mut self) -> Option<&mut TextEntries>  (the tree moves from the resolved slot to the stated fact on the first mutation, so it stands over a body set later)
  pub fn set_entries(&mut self, entries: Option<TextEntries>)  (stated, a tree or its absence stands over the payload's own)
  pub fn with_entries(mut self, entries: TextEntries) -> Self
  pub fn get_entry_by_path(&self, path: &FieldPath) -> Option<&TextEntry>
  pub fn entry_by_path(&self, path: &FieldPath) -> Result<&TextEntry>
  pub fn get_entry_by_path_mut(&mut self, path: &FieldPath) -> Option<&mut TextEntry>
  pub fn set_entry_by_path(&mut self, path: &FieldPath, value: TextBytes) -> Result<()>
  pub fn remove_entry_by_path(&mut self, path: &FieldPath) -> Option<TextEntry>
  pub fn mtime(&self) -> Result<Option<i64>>  (the header's `mtime` capture as an instant at the column's clock where it declares one and the line matched, else the handle's modification time, else none; a capture that is not an instant is a refusal naming the row, the object and the column)
  pub fn seqnum(&self) -> Result<u64>  (the row number under `start_rownum`, else the physical index; an explicitly stated event fact wins)
  pub fn state(&self) -> Result<&State>  (a `state` capture, else `00UNKNOWN`)
  pub fn creaunix(&self) -> Result<Option<i64>>  // and execunix, recdunix, refrecdunix, exprtime, prevunix, snapunix: the capture of that exact name as an instant, else none
  pub fn prevuuid(&self) -> Result<Option<Uuid>>  (a `prevuuid` capture, else none)
  pub fn event_fact(&self, column: EventColumn) -> Result<Option<Scalar>>  (what the line states under one of the nineteen event columns, as the column's cell: the capture-fed facts through the refusing readings above, so a capture the fact cannot read refuses by name; the rest through the trait doors)
  impl Element, Event for TextLine  (the readings above through the trait doors, a refusal read as the fact's default: `get_currunix` is `mtime`, the handle's time over a refused capture, the epoch where the line has none; `get_seqnum` the row number; `get_currhashcode` the XXH3-64 of the cross code, the row number and the body, so two identical bodies read at one instant stay apart; `get_crosscode` the `sourceuri`'s canonical text - the identifier the line was read under, which a located read spells as its URL and a named read spells as the name rather than where it resolved - else empty, with `crosshashcode` derived; `get_curruuid` `time_uuid` over microsecond time and that whole code, nil where the instant has no UUIDv7; `get_crossuuid` from the same cross hash; explicitly stated event facts win; `get_identifiers` the named captures the line matched, each under the capture's name; `get_parentuuids` and `get_srcuuids` what was stated, none otherwise; `is_after` by instant, then index; `finalize` lets the identity quartet derive again; `with_previous` is `Event::following`, `merge_with` is `Event::merging`)
  impl Clone, Debug, Display, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash for TextLine  (over the stated facts alone, never a resolved slot)

pub enum BytesType  (#[non_exhaustive]; Copy; Default = Binary; Binary, LargeBinary, BinaryView, LargeBinaryView, FixedBinary(u32), SizedBinary(u32); Display = the canonical name plus "(n)" on a numbered leaf - binary, large_binary_view, fixed_binary(16), sized_binary(16); serde as the name alone)
  pub const ALL: [Self; 6] = [ Self::Binary, Self::LargeBinary, Self::BinaryView, Self::LargeBinaryView, Self::FixedBinary(1), Self::SizedBinary(1), ];

### yggdryl::decimal::fixed  [rust/src/decimal.rs]
pub struct Decimal18  (one exact decimal at eighteen fractional digits and thirty-eight digits of precision - `decimal128(38, 18)` preapplied - as one i128 of units; Copy, Default (zero), Eq, Ord, Hash, Display (no trailing zero behind the point), Debug, FromStr; Add/Sub/Mul/Div and their assign forms refuse an overflow as the integers' do, Neg, Sum; From<i64>; From<Decimal18> for Decimal128 and for Scalar (a `Decimal128` at scale 18); re-exported at yggdryl::Decimal18)
  pub const SCALE: i8 = 18
  pub const PRECISION: u8 = 38
  pub const ZERO: Self; pub const ONE: Self; pub const MAX: Self; pub const MIN: Self  (thirty-eight nines, eighteen fractional, and its negation)
  pub const fn from_units(units: i128) -> Option<Self>  (the `decimal128(38, 18)` coefficient; None past the precision)
  pub const fn units(self) -> i128
  pub const fn from_int(value: i64) -> Self
  pub fn from_f64(value: f64) -> Option<Self>  (rounded to eighteen digits; None for a non-finite float or one past the precision)
  pub fn to_f64(self) -> f64
  pub fn parse(text: &str) -> Result<Self>  (lenient, one pass, no allocation: surrounding ASCII whitespace ignored, an empty text zero, an optional sign, grouping with `,` `_` `'` or a space ahead of the point, a leading or trailing point, digits past the eighteenth fractional one truncated toward zero, an `e` exponent; refuses text stating no number and a value past thirty-eight digits with Error::Parse)
  pub fn from_scalar(value: &Scalar) -> Option<Self>  (an exact decimal restated at eighteen digits, a whole integer, a finite float, or text `parse` reads)
  pub const fn dtype() -> DataType  (DataType::DECIMAL)
  pub const fn is_zero(self) -> bool; pub const fn is_negative(self) -> bool; pub const fn is_positive(self) -> bool; pub const fn abs(self) -> Self
  pub fn checked_add(self, other: Self) -> Option<Self>  // and checked_sub, checked_mul, checked_div: None past the precision, checked_div also for a divisor of nothing; mul and div keep eighteen digits and truncate the rest toward zero, widening to 256 bits for the one product
  pub fn truncated(self, places: u8) -> Self  (the value at `places` fractional digits, the rest truncated toward zero)
  pub const fn into_decimal128(self) -> Decimal128
impl DataType
  pub const DECIMAL: Self  (`decimal128(38, 18)`, what every Decimal18 is)

pub struct Uuid  (transparent u128, 16 bytes; big-endian storage; Copy, Eq/Ord/Hash, Display, serde; Value)
  pub const fn new(value: u128) -> Self
  pub fn from_v7(unix_micros: i64, payload: u64) -> Result<Self>  (RFC 9562 UUIDv7 over a microsecond instant, an exact packing rather than a fingerprint of one: 48-bit millisecond count, the microsecond within it in rand_a's high ten, then the whole 64-bit payload split two bits above the variant and sixty-two below; nothing is hashed or narrowed, so both facts read back out and two identifiers agree only where the microsecond and all 64 payload bits do; microsecond range 0..=281474976710655999; no allocation on success)
  pub const fn from_v8(payload: u128) -> Self  (replace version/variant bits only; preserve 122 payload bits; no allocation)
  pub fn from_bytes(value: &[u8]) -> Result<Self>
  pub const fn get(self) -> u128
  pub const fn is_nil(self) -> bool  (RFC 9562's all-zero identifier, `Default`: the identity no element has, and what an instant no UUIDv7 holds derives)
  pub const fn into_bytes(self) -> [u8; 16]

### yggdryl::scalar  [rust/src/scalar.rs]  (the Value and FamilyValue contracts a scalar's leaves answer are under yggdryl::value)
  pub enum Scalar  (#[non_exhaustive]; Null, Boolean, Int8, Int16, Int32, Int64, UInt8, UInt16, UInt32, UInt64, Int128, UInt128, Float16, Float32, Float64, Decimal32, Decimal64, Decimal128, Decimal256, Date32, Date64, Time32, Time64, DateTime64, Duration32, Duration64, Interval, String, Country, Currency, MicCode, CfiCode, Side, State, TimeInForce, IsinCode, CusipCode, SedolCode, BloombergCode, FIGICode, Uuid, Version, Url, Urn, Timezone, MimeType, MediaType, Bytes, Geometry, Geography, Sequence (Serie: a schema-free run or a column), Mapping, Struct, Variant (one Parquet Variant value: its metadata dictionary and its value payload), Arrow (feature arrow: Arc<ArrowScalar>, a columnar payload crossing as the scalar it is; into_native reads it as rows, as_arrow borrows it); 48 bytes; every variant holds its leaf directly, the twelve registered codes included; Eq/Ord/Hash across widths: integers by sign and magnitude, floats by the f64 reading, decimals normalized, temporals of one family by normalized count then zone)

### yggdryl::serie  [rust/src/serie.rs]  (Serie, the fourth side of the value model: many values, as a schema-free run or as the Arrow buffers of one Field; the family enums, the identity helpers and the serde shadow live here, the leaves in serie/)
  pub enum Serie  (#[non_exhaustive]; Run(Run), Null(Arc<NullSerie>), Boolean(Arc<BooleanSerie>), Integer(Arc<IntegerSerie>), Floating(Arc<FloatingSerie>), Decimal(Arc<DecimalSerie>), Temporal(Arc<TemporalSerie>), String(Arc<StringSerie>), Bytes(Arc<BytesSerie>), Struct(Arc<StructSerie>), Sequence(Arc<SequenceSerie>), Mapping(Arc<MappingSerie>), Union(Arc<UnionSerie>), Enum(Arc<EnumSerie>), RunEndEncoded(Arc<RunEndEncodedSerie>), Variant(Arc<VariantSerie>); 24 bytes; Clone is a pointer bump per column; Eq/Ord/Hash over the rows alone, never the leaf or the field, so a run and a column of equal rows are one value and hash alike; Display a run as its slice, a column as name[row, ...]; Debug a column as its leaf's Name { field, len, nulls }; Default = an empty run)
  pub fn new(values: impl Into<Arc<[Scalar]>>) -> Self  (a run)
  pub fn empty(field: Field) -> Result<Self>  (the empty column of field)
  pub fn with_capacity(field: Field, rows: usize) -> Result<Self>  (the empty column whose buffers reserve rows; an encoding reserves nothing)
  pub fn from_scalars(field: Field, rows: impl IntoIterator<Item = Scalar>) -> Result<Self>  (every row through Field::scalar, laid out once, taken through the door with no second proof)
  pub fn from_arrow_array(field: Field, array: ArrayRef) -> crate::arrow::Result<Self>  (the door: the layout proven against the field's projection, absence on the validity words, a value only where the layout is not the datatype's whole contract; a sliced list's offsets rebased; no buffer copied)
  pub fn from_arrow_batch(batch: &RecordBatch) -> crate::arrow::Result<Self>  (a record column named DEFAULT_ROOT_NAME whose children share the batch's columns)
  pub fn from_arrow_reader(reader: BatchReader) -> crate::arrow::Result<Self>  (drains the whole stream into one column: a column is one contiguous set of buffers, so the bound is the stream)
  pub fn into_arrow_batch(&self) -> crate::arrow::Result<RecordBatch>  (a record column only)
  pub fn into_arrow_reader(&self) -> crate::arrow::Result<BatchReader>  (one batch)
  pub fn field(&self) -> Option<&Field>  (None for a run)
  pub fn field_ref(&self) -> Option<&Arc<Field>>
  pub fn require_field(&self) -> Result<&Field>  (refuses a run by name)
  pub fn len(&self) -> usize  (constant)
  pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool
  pub fn null_count(&self) -> usize  (constant for a column, a walk for a run)
  pub fn is_null(&self, index: usize) -> Result<bool>  (refuses past the end naming the serie and both counts)
  pub fn scalar(&self, index: usize) -> Result<Scalar>  (one row built; a stored row never refuses, so the one refusal is an index past the end)
  pub fn get(&self, index: usize) -> Option<Cow<'_, Scalar>>  (borrowed for a run, built for a column)
  pub fn rows(&self) -> Cow<'_, [Scalar]>  (borrows a run, builds a column's rows)
  pub fn iter(&self) -> Children<'_>  (Children::Sequence for a run, Children::Column for a column; item Cow<'_, Scalar>)
  pub fn dtype(&self) -> Result<DataType>  (a column: a list of its field named item; a run: agreed out of its rows)
  pub fn as_run(&self) -> Option<&Run>
  pub fn as_slice(&self) -> Option<&[Scalar]>  (the run's values; None for a column)
  pub const fn is_column(&self) -> bool
  pub fn into_run(self) -> Run  (a run answers itself; a column builds its rows once)
  pub fn into_arrow_array(&self) -> Option<ArrayRef>  (None for a run; shares buffers)
  pub fn require_arrow_array(&self) -> Result<ArrayRef>  (refuses a run by name)
  pub fn slice(&self, offset: usize, length: usize) -> Result<Self>  (zero copy for a column, offsets rebased; a run copies its window; refuses past the end)
  pub fn child(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&Self>  (a record column's child by exact name)
  pub fn child_at(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&Self>
  pub fn children(&self) -> &[Self]  (a record or a union column's children; empty elsewhere)
  pub fn items(&self) -> Option<&Self>  (a sequence column's items, a mapping's entries, an encoding's values)
  pub fn get_child_by_path(&self, path: &FieldPath) -> Option<&Self>  (exactly DataType::get_field_by_path's segment semantics; an index, key, range or predicate segment reaches no column)
  pub fn splice(&mut self, range: Range<usize>, rows: Vec<Scalar>) -> Result<()>  (the one mutation: a column proves every row once, checks what a write could not do, then writes without failing; refuses a reversed range or one past the end naming the serie and both counts)
  pub fn set(&mut self, index: usize, value: Scalar) -> Result<()>  (a primitive or boolean column overwrites one slot in place)
  pub fn push(&mut self, value: Scalar) -> Result<()>  (amortized in place for a column; a run copies every value it holds)
  pub fn insert(&mut self, index: usize, value: Scalar) -> Result<()>
  pub fn remove(&mut self, index: usize) -> Result<Scalar>
  pub fn pop(&mut self) -> Result<Option<Scalar>>
  pub fn truncate(&mut self, len: usize) -> Result<()>
  pub fn clear(&mut self) -> Result<()>  (keeps the field)
  pub fn extend(&mut self, rows: Vec<Scalar>) -> Result<()>
  pub fn extend_from_serie(&mut self, other: &Self) -> Result<()>  (two columns whose fields have one datatype and whose nullability fits append buffer to buffer, no row read; anything else reads other's rows)
  pub fn resize(&mut self, len: usize, value: Scalar) -> Result<()>  (proves value once and writes clones)
  pub fn set_child(&mut self, child: Self) -> Result<()>  (a record column only: replaces the child of child's name or adds one, extending the field; refuses a run, a length other than len and a non-record column, each by name)
  pub fn set_cell(&mut self, path: &FieldPath, index: usize, value: Scalar) -> Result<()>  (a record column only: descends path through record children by name and writes one slot in place; refuses an absent row on the way)
  pub fn as_null(&self) -> Option<&NullSerie>  // and as_boolean, as_integer, as_floating, as_decimal, as_temporal, as_string, as_bytes, as_struct, as_sequence, as_mapping, as_union, as_enum, as_run_end_encoded, as_variant: the root variants, and as_int8 .. as_uint64, as_float16/32/64, as_decimal32/64/128/256, as_date32, as_date64, as_time32_second, as_time32_millisecond, as_time64_microsecond, as_time64_nanosecond, as_datetime_second/millisecond/microsecond/nanosecond, as_duration_second/millisecond/microsecond/nanosecond, as_interval_year_month/day_time/month_day_nano, as_utf8, as_large_utf8, as_utf8_view, as_binary_string, as_large_binary_string, as_binary_view_string, as_fixed_string, as_binary, as_large_binary, as_binary_view, as_fixed_bytes, as_list, as_large_list, as_list_view, as_large_list_view, as_fixed_size_list, as_dictionary: the named leaves; allocation free
  pub fn get_null_mut(&mut self) -> Option<&mut NullSerie>  // and get_<leaf>_mut for every as_<leaf> above: through Arc::make_mut, the leaf struct copied once when the column is shared; only the typed writers of a leaf can write through it, and they validate nullability
  impl Value for Serie
  impl NestedValue for Serie  (children answers Children::Sequence for a run and Children::Column for a column)
  impl Serialize for Serie  (a run as its values; a column as the private SerieWire, its field beside its rows)
  impl Deserialize for Serie  (the column wire alone; a list is refused naming the `serie` tag)
  impl From<Run> for Serie
  impl From<Serie> for Scalar
  impl From<IntegerSerie> for Serie  // and every family enum
  impl FromIterator<Scalar> for Serie  (a run)
  impl IntoIterator for &Serie  (item Cow<'_, Scalar>)
  pub enum IntegerSerie  (#[non_exhaustive]; Int8(Int8Serie) .. UInt64(UInt64Serie); emitted by serie_family!, as are the enums below: SerieValue dispatching to the leaf, Debug, identity over the rows, From<$family> for Serie)
  pub enum FloatingSerie  (Float16, Float32, Float64)
  pub enum DecimalSerie  (Decimal32, Decimal64, Decimal128, Decimal256)
  pub enum TemporalSerie  (Date32, Date64, Time32Second, Time32Millisecond, Time64Microsecond, Time64Nanosecond, DateTimeSecond, DateTimeMillisecond, DateTimeMicrosecond, DateTimeNanosecond, DurationSecond, DurationMillisecond, DurationMicrosecond, DurationNanosecond, IntervalYearMonth, IntervalDayTime, IntervalMonthDayNano)
  pub enum StringSerie  (Utf8(Utf8StringSerie), LargeUtf8, Utf8View, Binary(BinaryStringSerie), LargeBinary, BinaryView, Fixed(FixedStringSerie); the field says which string leaf or code the bytes are, the variant how they are laid out)
  pub enum BytesSerie  (Binary(BinarySerie), LargeBinary, BinaryView, Fixed(FixedBytesSerie))
  pub enum SequenceSerie  (List(ListSerie), LargeList(LargeListSerie), ListView(ListViewSerie), LargeListView(LargeListViewSerie), FixedSizeList(FixedSizeListSerie))
  pub fn items(&self) -> &Serie  (the item column under whichever cut this leaf is)
  pub enum EnumSerie  (Dictionary(DictionarySerie))

### yggdryl::serie::primitive  [rust/src/serie/primitive.rs]  (one column of fixed-width rows over every Arrow primitive type, and the 35 named leaves)
  pub trait PrimitiveLeaf: ArrowPrimitiveType + Sized + Send + Sync + 'static  (ties one Arrow primitive type to the leaf its column widens to)
  const NAME: &'static str
  fn into_serie(column: PrimitiveSerie<Self>) -> Serie
  fn from_serie(serie: &Serie) -> Option<&PrimitiveSerie<Self>>
  pub trait NativeLeaf: PrimitiveLeaf  (a leaf whose native domain is its datatype's whole domain, so a typed writer can take a native value: Int8 .. UInt64, Float16/32/64, Date32, DateTime, Duration and Interval; a decimal, Date64, Time32 or Time64 leaf is narrower than its storage and writes only through Scalar)
  pub struct PrimitiveSerie<T: PrimitiveLeaf>  (Clone is pointer bumps; Debug via debug_column; identity over the rows)
  pub fn values(&self) -> &[T::Native]  (the values buffer, lent)
  pub fn value(&self, index: usize) -> Option<T::Native>  (None when absent or past the end)
  pub fn nulls(&self) -> Option<&NullBuffer>
  pub const fn array(&self) -> &PrimitiveArray<T>
  pub fn push_value(&mut self, value: Option<T::Native>) -> Result<()>  (T: NativeLeaf, as are the three below; refuses None under a required field naming the column and leaves the column unchanged)
  pub fn set_value(&mut self, index: usize, value: Option<T::Native>) -> Result<()>  (one buffer write in place)
  pub fn splice_values(&mut self, range: Range<usize>, values: Vec<Option<T::Native>>) -> Result<()>
  pub fn extend_values(&mut self, values: Vec<Option<T::Native>>) -> Result<()>
  impl SerieValue for PrimitiveSerie<T>
  pub type Int8Serie = PrimitiveSerie<Int8Type>  // and Int16Serie, Int32Serie, Int64Serie, UInt8Serie, UInt16Serie, UInt32Serie, UInt64Serie, Float16Serie, Float32Serie, Float64Serie, Decimal32Serie, Decimal64Serie, Decimal128Serie, Decimal256Serie, Date32Serie, Date64Serie, Time32SecondSerie, Time32MillisecondSerie, Time64MicrosecondSerie, Time64NanosecondSerie, DateTimeSecondSerie, DateTimeMillisecondSerie, DateTimeMicrosecondSerie, DateTimeNanosecondSerie, DurationSecondSerie, DurationMillisecondSerie, DurationMicrosecondSerie, DurationNanosecondSerie, IntervalYearMonthSerie, IntervalDayTimeSerie, IntervalMonthDayNanoSerie: one alias per Arrow primitive type, each tied to its family variant

### yggdryl::serie::boolean  [rust/src/serie/boolean.rs]  (one column of booleans: a values bitmap and a validity bitmap)
  pub struct BooleanSerie

  pub fn value(&self, index: usize) -> Option<bool>
  pub fn nulls(&self) -> Option<&NullBuffer>

  pub fn set_value(&mut self, index: usize, value: Option<bool>) -> Result<()>  (one bit write in place)
  pub fn splice_values(&mut self, range: Range<usize>, values: Vec<Option<bool>>) -> Result<()>
  impl SerieValue for BooleanSerie

### yggdryl::serie::null  [rust/src/serie/null.rs]  (one column of nulls: a length, and no buffer at all)

  pub const fn push_value(&mut self)
  pub fn splice_values(&mut self, range: Range<usize>, count: usize) -> Result<()>  (range refusal only)
  impl SerieValue for NullSerie

### yggdryl::serie::bytes  [rust/src/serie/bytes.rs]  (every column whose rows are a run of bytes: offsets into one payload, views into several, or one fixed width; the marker says whether the field reads them as text or as bytes, and no byte leaf has a typed writer)
  pub trait ByteKind: Send + Sync + Clone + Copy + fmt::Debug + 'static  (which family a run of bytes belongs to when its layout belongs to both)
  pub struct Chars  (the marker for bytes a field reads as text)
  pub struct Octets  (the marker for bytes a field reads as bytes)
  pub trait ByteLeaf<K: ByteKind>: ByteArrayType + Sized + Send + Sync + 'static  (ties one offsets layout under one marker to its family variant: NAME, into_serie, from_serie)
  pub trait ViewLeaf<K: ByteKind>: ByteViewType + Sized + Send + Sync + 'static  (the same for a view layout)
  pub trait FixedLeaf: Sized  (the same for the fixed-width layout)
  pub struct ByteSerie<T: ByteArrayType, K: ByteKind>  (offsets into one payload buffer; Utf8, LargeUtf8, Binary, LargeBinary under Chars or Octets)
  pub fn offsets(&self) -> &OffsetBuffer<T::Offset>
  pub fn payload(&self) -> &Buffer
  pub fn nulls(&self) -> Option<&NullBuffer>
  pub const fn array(&self) -> &GenericByteArray<T>
  pub fn value(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&T::Native>  (None when absent or past the end)
  impl SerieValue for ByteSerie<T, K>
  pub struct ByteViewSerie<T: ByteViewType, K: ByteKind>  (views into several payload buffers; a write repacks every run)
  pub fn views(&self) -> &[u128]
  pub fn payloads(&self) -> &[Buffer]
  pub fn nulls(&self) -> Option<&NullBuffer>
  pub const fn array(&self) -> &GenericByteViewArray<T>
  pub fn value(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&T::Native>
  impl SerieValue for ByteViewSerie<T, K>
  pub struct FixedSerie<K: ByteKind>  (one payload buffer and no offsets; a same-count overwrite is one memcpy at index * width)
  pub fn width(&self) -> i32
  pub fn payload(&self) -> &Buffer
  pub fn nulls(&self) -> Option<&NullBuffer>
  pub const fn array(&self) -> &FixedSizeBinaryArray
  pub fn value(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&[u8]>
  impl SerieValue for FixedSerie<K>
  pub type BinarySerie = ByteSerie<BinaryType, Octets>
  pub type LargeBinarySerie = ByteSerie<LargeBinaryType, Octets>
  pub type BinaryViewSerie = ByteViewSerie<BinaryViewType, Octets>
  pub type FixedBytesSerie = FixedSerie<Octets>  (a UUID, or any identity stored at one width)

### yggdryl::serie::string  [rust/src/serie/string.rs]  (the string family's leaves over the byte layouts: the field says which of the eighteen string leaves or twelve codes the bytes are)
  pub type Utf8StringSerie = ByteSerie<Utf8Type, Chars>
  pub type LargeUtf8StringSerie = ByteSerie<LargeUtf8Type, Chars>
  pub type Utf8ViewStringSerie = ByteViewSerie<StringViewType, Chars>
  pub type BinaryStringSerie = ByteSerie<BinaryType, Chars>  (a windows-1252 or other non-UTF-8 charset, stored as bytes)
  pub type LargeBinaryStringSerie = ByteSerie<LargeBinaryType, Chars>
  pub type BinaryViewStringSerie = ByteViewSerie<BinaryViewType, Chars>
  pub type FixedStringSerie = FixedSerie<Chars>

### yggdryl::serie::structure  [rust/src/serie/structure.rs]  (one column of records: one child column per child field beside the record's validity; no child is mutably reachable, so alignment cannot break)
  pub struct StructSerie
  pub fn children(&self) -> &[Serie]
  pub fn child(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&Serie>  (by exact name, as Field::index_of resolves it)
  pub fn child_at(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&Serie>
  pub const fn nulls(&self) -> Option<&NullBuffer>
  pub fn set_child(&mut self, child: Serie) -> Result<()>  (replaces the child of child's name or adds one, extending the field; refuses a run and a length other than len)
  pub fn set_cell(&mut self, path: &FieldPath, index: usize, value: Scalar) -> Result<()>  (one slot written in place, path deep; refuses an absent row on the way: "row {index} of {name} is absent; set the whole row")
  pub fn without_child(&self, name: &str) -> Result<Self>  (a new column sharing every other child)
  impl SerieValue for StructSerie

### yggdryl::serie::sequence  [rust/src/serie/sequence.rs]  (the five list layouts: an offsets cut, a viewed cut kept compact, or width items per row, each over one item column; every cut is rebased)
  pub trait ListLeaf: OffsetSizeTrait + Send + Sync + 'static  (ties one offset width to the offsets leaf and the viewed leaf it widens through)
  pub struct OffsetListSerie<O: OffsetSizeTrait>

  pub type LargeListSerie = OffsetListSerie<i64>

  pub const fn nulls(&self) -> Option<&NullBuffer>
  pub fn range(&self, index: usize) -> Option<Range<usize>>  (the item range row index occupies; None when absent or past the end)
  pub fn row(&self, index: usize) -> Option<Serie>  (the item column sliced to that row, zero copy)
  impl SerieValue for OffsetListSerie<O>
  pub struct OffsetListViewSerie<O: OffsetSizeTrait>  (views compact: row i starts where row i - 1 ended; the door rebases a foreign layout onto exactly the items it reaches)
  pub type ListViewSerie = OffsetListViewSerie<i32>
  pub type LargeListViewSerie = OffsetListViewSerie<i64>
  pub const fn items(&self) -> &Serie
  pub const fn offsets(&self) -> &ScalarBuffer<O>
  pub const fn sizes(&self) -> &ScalarBuffer<O>
  pub const fn nulls(&self) -> Option<&NullBuffer>
  pub fn range(&self, index: usize) -> Option<Range<usize>>
  pub fn row(&self, index: usize) -> Option<Serie>
  impl SerieValue for OffsetListViewSerie<O>
  pub struct FixedSizeListSerie  (width items per row and no cut; a null row holds width placeholder items and a cleared bit)
  pub const fn width(&self) -> usize
  pub const fn items(&self) -> &Serie
  pub const fn nulls(&self) -> Option<&NullBuffer>
  pub fn range(&self, index: usize) -> Option<Range<usize>>
  pub fn row(&self, index: usize) -> Option<Serie>
  impl SerieValue for FixedSizeListSerie

### yggdryl::value  [rust/src/value/mod.rs]  (what a datatype, a field and a value each owe the root that holds them, and what a family owes its leaves; the module is private and every name here is re-exported at the crate root as yggdryl::<Name>)
pub trait Value: Sized + Clone + fmt::Debug + fmt::Display + Eq + Ord + Hash + Send + Sync + 'static  (one concrete scalar representation; no associated consts: a leaf's identity is its dtype; no Family type - a leaf widens through into_scalar)
  fn dtype(&self) -> Result<DataType>
  fn into_scalar(self) -> Scalar
  fn from_scalar(value: &Scalar) -> Option<&Self>
  fn into_variant(&self) -> Result<Variant>  (encode through Scalar using the Parquet Variant mapping)
  fn from_variant(value: &Variant) -> Result<Self>  (decode and require this exact leaf; Variant itself shares its buffers)
pub trait FamilyValue: Sized + Clone + fmt::Debug + fmt::Display + Eq + Ord + Hash + Send + Sync + 'static  (what a family's value enum owes: it stands for any one leaf of the family; implemented by Integer, Floating, Decimal, Temporal, Code, Geospatial and Nested)
  const KIND: DataTypeKind
  fn dtype(&self) -> Result<DataType>
  fn into_scalar(self) -> Scalar
  fn from_scalar(value: &Scalar) -> Option<Self>  (by value: the scalar holds the leaf and not the family, and every leaf is Copy or one shared pointer)
pub trait IntegerValue: Value  (implemented by Int8, Int16, Int32, Int64, UInt8, UInt16, UInt32, UInt64, Int128 and UInt128)
  const SIGNED: bool
  const BIT_WIDTH: u8
  fn as_i128(&self) -> Option<i128>
  fn as_u128(&self) -> Option<u128>
  fn from_i128(value: i128) -> Result<Self>
pub trait FloatingValue: Value  (implemented by Float16, Float32 and Float64)
  const BIT_WIDTH: u8
  fn as_f64(&self) -> f64
pub trait DecimalValue: Value  (implemented by Decimal32, Decimal64, Decimal128 and Decimal256)
  fn coefficient(&self) -> i256
  fn scale(&self) -> i8
  fn rescale(self, scale: i8) -> Result<Self>
pub trait TemporalValue: Value  (implemented by Date32, Date64, Time32, Time64, DateTime64, Duration32, Duration64 and Interval)
  const FAMILY: &'static str  // date, time, datetime, duration, interval
  const BIT_WIDTH: u8
  fn count(&self) -> i64
  fn unit(&self) -> TimeUnit
  fn timezone(&self) -> Timezone
  fn with_unit(self, unit: TimeUnit) -> Result<Self>
  fn with_timezone(self, timezone: Timezone) -> Result<Self>
pub trait GeospatialValue: Value  (implemented by Geometry and Geography)
  fn as_bytes(&self) -> &[u8]
  fn storage(&self) -> &Arc<[u8]>
pub trait CodeValue: Value  (implemented by the twelve registered codes)
  const WIDTH: usize
  fn as_str(&self) -> &str
  fn storage(&self) -> &SmolStr
  fn merge_with(self, other: &Self) -> Self  (provided as this code; the better statement of two codes of one kind: CfiCode fills every `X` from the other where the two describe one instrument, State that reached none takes the other and otherwise the further along stands, Side `UNKNOWN`, Currency `XXX` and MicCode `XXXX` take the other, every identifier stands as it is)
pub trait NestedValue: Value  (implemented by Run, Serie, Mapping and Struct)
  fn len(&self) -> usize
  fn is_empty(&self) -> bool  (provided: len() == 0)
  fn children(&self) -> Children<'_>
pub enum Nested  (Sequence(Serie), Mapping(Mapping), Struct(Struct), Variant(Variant); the nested family as one value, every variant named as the leaf and the Scalar variant are; Clone, Eq/Ord/Hash, Debug, Display = the leaf's)
  impl FamilyValue for Nested  (KIND = DataTypeKind::Nested)
  impl From<Serie>, From<Mapping>, From<Struct> for Nested
  impl From<Nested> for Scalar
pub trait FieldSidecar: Clone + fmt::Debug + Default + Eq + Ord + Hash + Send + Sync + Sized + 'static  (the per-column facts only one datatype has; `()` for every datatype but the dictionary-encoded one)
  fn dictionary_id(&self) -> Option<i64>  (provided: None)
  fn dictionary_is_ordered(&self) -> Option<bool>  (provided: None)
  fn set_dictionary_options(&mut self, id: i64, is_ordered: bool) -> Result<bool>  (provided: refused, "dictionary options require a dictionary datatype")
  impl FieldSidecar for ()
pub struct DictionaryOptions  (Arrow's IPC dictionary identifier and ordering flag, one column's encoding rather than its datatype; Copy, Default, Eq/Ord/Hash; FieldSidecar answering both and setting both, reporting whether anything changed)
pub trait DataTypeValue: Clone + fmt::Debug + fmt::Display + Eq + Ord + Hash + Send + Sync + Sized + 'static  (one datatype: a family's payload, or the root that redirects to it; DataType itself implements it with FAMILY = "datatype" and Sidecar = DictionaryOptions)
  const FAMILY: &'static str  (the family's parameter-free name, as a binding and a refusal spell it)
  type Sidecar: FieldSidecar
  fn id(&self) -> DataTypeId
  fn kind(&self) -> DataTypeKind
  fn validate(&self) -> Result<()>
  fn into_dtype(self) -> DataType
  fn from_dtype(dtype: &DataType) -> Option<Self>  (owned: a payload is Copy or one shared pointer)
  fn cast_scalar(&self, value: &crate::Scalar) -> Result<crate::Scalar>  (provided, as are the four below: the datatype is the target, and every family answers the doors through the root's one recursive walk)
  fn encode_value_stream_bytes(&self, value: &crate::Scalar) -> Result<crate::ValueStream>  (provided: the value cast to this datatype and encoded one chunk per leaf; encode_value_bytes, decode_value_bytes and decode_value_stream_bytes beside it, each through the datatype the leaf widens to)
  fn cast_arrow_array(&self, array: arrow_array::ArrayRef, options: crate::ArrowCastOptions) -> crate::arrow::Result<arrow_array::ArrayRef>
  fn cast_arrow_scalar(&self, array: arrow_array::ArrayRef, options: crate::ArrowCastOptions) -> crate::arrow::Result<arrow_array::Scalar<arrow_array::ArrayRef>>
  fn cast_arrow_batch(&self, batch: arrow_array::RecordBatch, options: crate::ArrowCastOptions) -> crate::arrow::Result<arrow_array::RecordBatch>
  fn cast_arrow_reader(&self, reader: crate::arrow::BatchReader, options: crate::ArrowCastOptions) -> crate::arrow::Result<crate::arrow::BatchReader>
pub trait FieldValue<D: DataTypeValue>: Clone + fmt::Debug + fmt::Display + Sized  (one field over one family's datatype; implemented by every FieldOf<D> and by Field as FieldValue<DataType>)
  fn name(&self) -> &str
  fn dtype(&self) -> DataType
  fn typed_dtype(&self) -> D
  fn is_nullable(&self) -> bool
  fn metadata(&self) -> &Metadata
  fn validate(&self) -> Result<()>
  fn into_field(self) -> Field
  fn from_field(field: &Field) -> Option<&Self>

pub struct ColumnRows<'a>  (the rows of one column, built one at a time as the walk reaches them; a stored row is one its field accepts, so none refuses)
pub trait SerieValue: Clone + fmt::Debug + fmt::Display + Eq + Ord + Hash + Send + Sync + Sized + 'static  (what every column leaf of Serie and every family enum owes: its field, its length, one row built on demand, a zero-copy slice, the one mutation splice - prove once, check once, write without failing - its buffers as an Arrow array, and the widening to and narrowing from Serie; Serie itself does not implement it, the run having no field)
  fn field(&self) -> &Field

  pub const ALL: [Self; 18] = [ Self::Utf8String, Self::LargeUtf8String, Self::Utf8StringView, Self::LargeUtf8StringView, Self::FixedUtf8String(1), Self::SizedUtf8String(1), Self::AsciiString, Self::LargeAsciiString, Self::AsciiStringView, Self::LargeAsciiStringView, Self::FixedAsciiString(1), Self::SizedAsciiString(1), Self::Cp1252String, Self::LargeCp1252String, Self::Cp1252StringView, Self::LargeCp1252StringView, Self::FixedCp1252String(1), Self::SizedCp1252String(1), ];

  pub const fn charset(self) -> Charset  // Utf8, Ascii or Cp1252

  pub const fn is_view(self) -> bool
  pub const fn is_large(self) -> bool

  pub const fn same_shape_as(self, other: Self) -> bool  (the same charset and storage shape, the number ignored)
  pub fn with_bound(self, bound: u32) -> Result<Self>  (Fixed* keeps its shape; a plain or Sized* leaf becomes Sized*(n) of its charset; the large and view leaves refuse)
  pub fn with_declared_bound(self, bound: Option<u32>) -> Result<Self>  (None on a numbered leaf refused)
  pub fn with_charset(self, charset: Charset) -> Result<Self>  (the same shape in another charset's family, the number kept; a charset with no leaf refused)
  pub const fn validate(self) -> Result<()>  (a number of 0 refused)

  pub fn from_str(value: &str) -> Result<Self>  (from_spelling over the folded text)
  pub fn from_spelling(word: &str) -> Option<Self>  (every alias, folded: general_spelling plus the charset-named ones - utf8, largeutf8, utf8view, fixedutf8, sizedutf8, ascii, usascii, cp1252, windows1252, ...)
  pub fn general_spelling(word: &str) -> Option<Self>  (the charset-free spellings alone - string, str, text, varchar, char, fixedstring, stringview, largestring, largestringview, sizedstring - naming the UTF-8 leaf of their shape; the only spellings a charset may stand beside)
  pub fn from_declaration(layout: &str, charset: Option<&str>, bound: Option<u32>) -> Result<Self>  (what both bindings call: the spelling, a charset only beside a general spelling, then with_declared_bound)

### yggdryl::uri::datatype  [rust/src/uri/datatype.rs]  (the `uri` family: one leaf per shape an identifier takes - `url`, a location, and `urn`, a name - each value held behind one Arc, stored as Utf8 under the extension name `yggdryl.url` / `yggdryl.urn`, every text cell parsed and canonicalized on the way in, a cell reading as neither refused by field and row)
pub enum UriType  (#[non_exhaustive]; Url (the default), Urn; Copy; the payload of DataType::Uri(UriType))
  pub const ALL: [Self; 2] = [Self::Url, Self::Urn];
  pub const fn id(self) -> DataTypeId
  pub const fn from_id(id: DataTypeId) -> Option<Self>
  pub const fn as_str(self) -> &'static str
  pub const fn family(self) -> &'static str  (`uri`)
  pub const fn validate(self) -> Result<()>
  impl DataTypeValue for UriType  (FAMILY `uri`, kind Text, no sidecar)
  impl From<UriType> for DataType
  pub type UriField = FieldOf<UriType>;  (the alias is declared in field.rs with the other leaves; `UriField::new(name, UriType::Url, nullable)`)
impl DataType
  pub const fn url() -> Self
  pub const fn urn() -> Self
  pub const fn uri_type(&self) -> Option<UriType>
  impl Value for Url  (dtype `url`; Scalar::Url(Arc<Url>))
  impl From<Url> for Scalar
  impl Value for Urn  (dtype `urn`; Scalar::Urn(Arc<Urn>))
  impl From<Urn> for Scalar
impl Scalar
  pub fn as_uri(&self) -> Option<&Uri>  (the identifier a url or a urn scalar holds: the one Uri both leaves narrow)
